country_event = {
	id = pkk_attack.1

	title = pkk_attack.1.t
	desc = pkk_attack.1.d
	picture = GFX_pkk_ambush
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		tag = TUR
		has_idea = pkk_terror_idea
		TUR = {
			owns_state = 163
			controls_state = 163
			owns_state = 162
			controls_state = 162
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 300
		modifier = {
			factor = 1 #lower the value, event becomes more scarce
			check_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 10 compare = less_than_or_equals }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.9
			check_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 10 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 20 compare = less_than_or_equals }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.8
			check_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 20 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 30 compare = less_than_or_equals }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.7
			check_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 30 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 40 compare = less_than_or_equals }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.6
			check_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 40 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 50 compare = less_than_or_equals }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.5
			check_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 50 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 60 compare = less_than_or_equals }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.4
			check_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 60 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 70 compare = less_than_or_equals }

			factor = 0.3

			check_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 80 compare = less_than_or_equals }
		}

			factor = 0.2
			check_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 80 compare = greater_than }

			factor = 0.1 #higher the value, event becomes more frequent
			check_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 90 compare = greater_than }

		}
	}

	immediate = {
		save_event_target_as = pkk_attack_thingy
	}

	option = {
		name = pkk_attack.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: pkk_attack.1.a executed"
		event_target:pkk_attack_thingy = {
			hidden_effect = {
				random_list = {
					40 = { #attack failed
					}
					60 = { #attack succeed
						random_list = {
							50 = { #infrastructure got attacked
								country_event = { id = pkk_attack.2 hours = 6 }
								set_country_flag = pkk_inf_attack
								random_list = { #decides which state got attacked and the amount of damage
									60 = {
										random_list = {
											40 = {
												162 = { damage_building = { type = infrastructure damage = 0.3 } }
												random_list = {
													20 = {
														decrease_popularity_1 = yes

														add_stability = -0.01
													}
												}
											}
											30 = {
												162 = { damage_building = { type = infrastructure damage = 0.5 } }
												random_list = {
													20 = {
														decrease_popularity_2 = yes

														add_stability = -0.01
													}
												}
											}
											25 = {

												random_list = {

														decrease_popularity_2 = yes

country_event = {

	id = pkk_attack.2

	title = pkk_attack.2.t
	desc = pkk_attack.2.d
	picture = GFX_pkk_ambush

	fire_only_once = yes

	trigger = {
		tag = TUR
		has_idea = pkk_terror_idea
		has_country_flag = pkk_inf_attack
	}

	immediate = {
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName] pkk inf attack happened"
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.25
			tag = TUR
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = pkk_attack.2.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: pkk_attack.2.a executed"
		clr_country_flag = pkk_inf_attack
		hidden_effect = {
			random_list = { #free airstrike because of recent attack
				5 = {	# airstrike failed
				}
				95 = {	# success
					60 = { #60 chance of reducing by 1
						subtract_from_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 2 }
					}
					40 = {
						subtract_from_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 4 }
					}
					random_list = {
						5 = { #assassination of a field commander
							subtract_from_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 5 }
						}
					}
					random_list = {
						15 = { #making a good propaganda about figthing back
							add_1_popularity = yes

						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = pkk_attack.2.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: pkk_attack.2.b executed"

		add_stability = 0.01
		add_timed_idea = { idea = treat_wounds days = 5 }
		clr_country_flag = pkk_inf_attack
	}
}

country_event = { #pkk will increase by 3 every 2 month no matter what, because we need a dick move
	id = pkk_increase.1
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_idea = pkk_terror_idea
		tag = TUR
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 60 }

	option = {
		name = pkk_increase.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: pkk_increase.1.a executed"
		add_to_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 3 }
		clamp_variable = { var = pkk_presence max = pkk_presence_max }
	}
}

country_event = { #hidden event that increases pkk power if you haven't done 4 or more counter terror operations last 4 months

	id = pkk_increase.2
	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_idea = pkk_terror_idea
		tag = TUR
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 120 }
	option = {
		name = pkk_increase.2.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: pkk_increase.2.a executed"
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ct_op_count_1 value = 4 compare = less_than } }
			add_to_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 5 }
			clamp_variable = { var = pkk_presence max = pkk_presence_max }
			set_variable = { var = ct_op_count_1 value = 0 }
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName] pkk power increased"
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { var = ct_op_count_1 value = 0 }
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName] pkk power stays"
		}
	}
}

country_event = { #hidden event that increases pkk power even more if you haven't done any operations in the last 10 months
	id = pkk_increase.3

	hidden = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_idea = pkk_terror_idea
		tag = TUR
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 300 }

	option = {
		name = pkk_increase.3.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: pkk_increase.3.a executed"
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = ct_op_count_2 value = 0 compare = equals } }
			add_to_variable = { var = pkk_presence value = 10 }
			clamp_variable = { var = pkk_presence max = pkk_presence_max }
			set_variable = { var = ct_op_count_2 value = 0 }
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName] pkk power increased a lot"
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { var = ct_op_count_2 value = 0 }
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName] pkk power stays"
		}
	}
}

country_event = {

	id = western_protest.1

	title = western_protest.1.t
	desc = western_protest.1.d

	#mean_time_to_happen = { days = 2 }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { tag = TUR }
		has_country_flag = they_hatin
	}

	option = {
		name = western_protest.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName] country protested"
		clr_country_flag = they_hatin
		TUR = { add_timed_idea = { idea = protest_thingy days = 90 } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				ROOT = {
					has_opinion = {
						target = TUR
						value < 40 #more likely to protest if relations are bad
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = western_protest.1.b
		log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName] country didnt protested"
		clr_country_flag = they_hatin
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 1
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				ROOT = {
					has_opinion = {
						target = TUR
						value > 40 #less likely to protest if relations are good
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}


country_event = { # Prevents HDP from getting majority
	id = rebalance_kurdish_politics.1
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = TUR
		check_variable = { party_pop_array^8 > 0.20 }
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 30 }

	option = {
		name = rebalance_kurdish_politics.1.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: rebalance_kurdish_politics.1.a executed"
		set_temp_variable = { kurdchange = party_pop_array^8 }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { kurdchange = 0.12 }

		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { var = party_pop_array^8 value = 0.12 }
			add_to_variable = { var = party_pop_array^5 value = kurdchange }
		}
	}
}

country_event = { # Prevents HDP from ruling party leader
	id = rebalance_kurdish_politics.2
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		original_tag = TUR
		has_country_leader_with_trait = emerging_Mod_Vilayat_e_Faqih
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = { days = 30 }

	option = {
		name = rebalance_kurdish_politics.2.a
		log = "[GetDateText]: [This.GetName]: rebalance_kurdish_politics.2.a executed"
		hidden_effect = {
			clear_array = ruling_party
			clear_array = gov_coalition_array
			add_to_array = { ruling_party = 5 }
			update_government_coalition_strength = yes
			update_party_name = yes
			set_coalition_drift = yes

			kill_country_leader = yes
		}
	}
}



####################### Turkey Submod ###########################

##Event: Operation Of Return To Life
country_event = {
 id = Turkey.1
 title = Turkey.1.title
 desc = Turkey.1.desc
 picture = GFX_hayat
  fire_only_once = yes

    trigger = {
         tag = TUR
		 NOT = {
		 has_government = fascism
		 has_capitulated = yes
		 }
         AND = {
             date > 2000.1.1
             date < 2000.2.1
         }
    }

    mean_time_to_happen = {days = 1}

	option = {
		name = Turkey.1.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}
    
	add_political_power = -15

##Event: MHP Reaction To Military
country_event = {
 id = Turkey.293
 title = Turkey.293.title
 desc = Turkey.293.desc
 picture = GFX_coup_tanks
 fire_only_once = yes

    trigger = {
         tag = TUR
         has_completed_focus = TUR_left_armed_forces
         nationalist_right_wing_populists_are_in_power = yes		 
    }

    mean_time_to_happen = {days = 1}

	option = {
		name = Turkey.293.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}

swap_ideas = { 
  remove_idea = turkish_military_infulence
  add_idea = turkish_military_infulence_coup
}

	add_to_array = { ruling_party = 4 }
	
		hidden_effect = {
			clear_array = gov_coalition_array
			clear_array = ruling_party
			add_to_array = { ruling_party = max_index_1 }
		}

		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				set_country_flag = [META_SET_PARTY1]
			}
			META_SET_PARTY1 = "[meta_set_ideology1]"
		}

		set_leader = yes #set in desicion which party is selected
		update_government_coalition_strength = yes
		clear_coalition_count_ideas = yes

			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { party_pop_array^22 = 1.0 }
				recalculate_party = yes
			}

			create_country_leader = {
				name = "İlker Başbuğ"
				picture = "ktu.dds"
				ideology = Nat_Autocracy
				traits = {
					nationalist_Nat_Autocracy
					military_career
				}
			}
		
						add_popularity = {
							ideology = nationalist
							popularity = 0.45
						}	
                set_politics = {
                ruling_party = nationalist
                elections_allowed = no
            }	
	}	
  }

 title = Turkey.590.title
 desc = Turkey.590.desc
 picture = GFX_sultanahmet_attack
  fire_only_once = yes

    trigger = {
         tag = TUR
		has_idea = TUR_PKK_insurgency_active
         AND = {
             date > 2016.1.12
	         date < 2016.2.12
         }
    }

    mean_time_to_happen = {days = 1}

	option = {
		name = Turkey.590.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}
    
		add_stability = -0.01
		934 = {
			damage_building = {
				type = infrastructure
				damage = 1.0
			}
			damage_building = {
				type = infrastructure
				damage = 1.0
			}
        }
	}
}

##Event: US-Anti Air Investments
country_event = {
 id = Turkey.597
 title = Turkey.597.title
 desc = Turkey.597.desc
 picture = GFX_kurecik_radar_base
  fire_only_once = yes

    trigger = {
         tag = USA
         AND = {
             date > 2012.2.7
	         date < 2012.2.7
         }
    }

    mean_time_to_happen = {days = 1}

	option = {
		name = Turkey.597.b
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}
    
	}


	option = {
		name = Turkey.597.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}
    
	subtract_from_variable = { treasury = 15.0 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = 15_billion_expense_tt
				set_temp_variable = { int_investment_change = 15.0 }
				modify_international_investment_effect = yes
		reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = TUR modifier = kurecik_base }
	army_experience = 25
	TUR = {
	country_event = Turkey.598
	}
	PER = {
	country_event = Turkey.599
        }
} }


##Event: US-Anti Air Investments (Turkey)
country_event = {
 id = Turkey.598
 title = Turkey.598.title
 desc = Turkey.598.desc
 picture = GFX_kurecik_radar_base
 is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = Turkey.598.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}
    
		add_stability = -0.01
		162 = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = anti_air_building
					instant_build = yes
					level = 1
				}
				add_building_construction = {
					type = radar_station
					instant_build = yes
					level = 1
				}
				add_building_construction = {
					type = fuel_silo
					instant_build = yes
					level = 1
				}
	}
} }

 id = Turkey.610
 title = Turkey.610.title
 desc = Turkey.610.desc
 picture = GFX_lira
 fire_only_once = yes

    trigger = {
         tag = TUR
		  AND = {
             date > 2023.6.22
             date < 2023.7.22
         }
neutrality_Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_are_in_power = yes
		 NOT = {
		 has_government = fascism
		 has_global_flag = TUR_MAFIATIC_TR
         has_global_flag = ERDOGAN_NOT_ACHIEVED
		 has_capitulated = yes
		 } 
    }

	option = {
		name = Turkey.610.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}

     add_stability = -0.05
	set_variable = { TUR_interest_rate = 15.0 }
	set_variable = { TUR_amount_lira_dollar = 25.24  }
	set_variable = { TUR_lira_amount_against_euro = 27.53 }
	set_variable = { TUR_lira_amount_against_sterlin = 32.09 }
	set_variable = { TUR_lira_amount_against_bitcoin = 803729 }
  }  }

 id = TurkeyF.1
 title = TurkeyF.1.title
 desc = TurkeyF.1.desc
 picture = GFX_coup_success_altermath
 is_triggered_only = yes

    trigger = {
    }

    mean_time_to_happen = {days = 1}

	option = {
		name = TurkeyF.1.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}
   
			hidden_effect = { country_event = Turkey_news.9 } 
add_named_threat = { threat = 3 name = TURKISH_REVOLUTION_TSK }
set_country_flag = do_not_kick_me
	add_stability = -0.03
	add_political_power = -75
set_global_flag = DO_NOT_KICK_ME_NATO_TR
swap_ideas = { 
  remove_idea = turkish_military_infulence
  add_idea = turkish_military_infulence_coup
}

			load_focus_tree = MD_TUR_MIL
    custom_effect_tooltip = MIL_COUP

			clear_array = ruling_party
			clear_array = gov_coalition_array
			add_to_array = { ruling_party = 22 } #Nat_Autocracy
			add_to_variable = { party_pop_array^22 = 0.5 } #Nat_Autocracy
			recalculate_party = yes
			IF = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_government = nationalist
					}
				}
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = nationalist
					popularity = 0.5
				}
				recalculate_party = yes
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = nationalist
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_ruling_leader = yes
				set_leader = yes
			}
		


			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Hüseyin Kivirikoglu"
				picture = "huseyin_kivnikoglu.dds"
				ideology = Nat_Autocracy
				traits = {
					nationalist_Nat_Autocracy
					military_career
				}
			}
                set_politics = {
                ruling_party = nationalist
                elections_allowed = no
            }						
	}
}

} 
	}

##Event: Buy Islands
country_event = {
 id = TurkeyF.29
 title = TurkeyF.29.title
 desc = TurkeyF.29.desc
 picture = GFX_report_event_Money_ITA_3
 is_triggered_only = yes

    trigger = {
    }

    mean_time_to_happen = {days = 1}

	option = {
		name = TurkeyF.29.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}
    
	add_stability = -0.05
	add_political_power = -50	

			subtract_from_variable = { treasury = -100.0 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = 100_billion_revenue_tt
		reverse_add_opinion_modifier = { target = TUR modifier = island_deal }
        add_opinion_modifier = { target = TUR modifier = island_deal }			
	TUR = {
	transfer_state = 143
			subtract_from_variable = { treasury = 100.0 }
	add_stability = 0.01
	add_political_power = 25
	} } 

	option = {
		name = TurkeyF.29.b
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}
    
	add_stability = 0.03
	add_political_power = 25
	TUR = {
	country_event = TurkeyF.30
	  }
	}
}

##Event: Elect A Khan
country_event = {
 id = TurkeyF.46
 title = TurkeyF.46.title
 desc = TurkeyF.46.desc
 picture = GFX_turkic_khan
 is_triggered_only = yes

    trigger = {
    }

    mean_time_to_happen = {days = 1}

    #Bahceli
	option = {
		name = TurkeyF.46.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 100}
    
	add_political_power = 75
    add_stability = 0.05
	add_war_support = 0.05
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Devlet Bahçeli"
				picture = "Devlet_Bahceli.dds"
				ideology = Nat_Populism
				traits = {
					nationalist_Nat_Populism
				}
			}
	}

    #Aliyev Family
	option = {
		name = TurkeyF.46.b
		ai_chance = { factor = 100}
    
	add_political_power = 25
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Heydar Aliyev"
				picture = "Heydar_Aliyev.dds"
				ideology = conservatism
				traits = {
					western_conservatism
				}
			}
	}

    #Erdogan
	option = {
		name = TurkeyF.46.c
		ai_chance = { factor = 100}
    
	add_war_support = 0.01
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Recep Tayyip Erdogan"
				picture = "Recep_Tayyip_Erdogan.dds"
				ideology = Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood
				traits = {
					neutrality_Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood
					career_politician
				}
			}
	}
	
    #Kazakh Leadership
	option = {
		name = TurkeyF.46.d
		ai_chance = { factor = 100}
    
	add_political_power = 100
    add_stability = -0.01
			create_country_leader = {
				name = "Serikbolsyn Abdildin"
				picture = "abdildin.dds"
				ideology = Communist-State
				traits = {
					emerging_Communist-State
				}
			}
	}
}

		subtract_from_variable = { TUR_union_acceptance = 5 }

	custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_GREEN_relations_1
	custom_effect_tooltip = TUR_LIB_relations_1
	custom_effect_tooltip = GREENS_DECREASE_3
	set_global_flag = AUGUST_PROTEST_BACK_UP
			subtract_from_variable = { TUR_rel_akp = 3 }
			subtract_from_variable = { TUR_rel_chp = -7 }
			subtract_from_variable = { TUR_rel_green = -6 }

				add_to_variable = { party_pop_array^12 = -0.01 }

			}
 }

			neutrality_Neutral_conservatism
			staunch_constitutionalist
		}
	}
		clear_array = ruling_party
			clear_array = gov_coalition_array
			add_to_array = { ruling_party = 14 } #Neutral_conservatism
			add_to_variable = { party_pop_array^14 = 0.5 } #Neutral_conservatism
			recalculate_party = yes
			IF = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_government = neutrality
					}
				}
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = neutrality
					popularity = 0.5
				}
				recalculate_party = yes
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = neutrality
				}
			}
			else = {
				meta_effect = {
					text = {
						set_country_flag = [META_SET_RULING_PARTY]
					}
					META_SET_RULING_PARTY = "[meta_set_ruling_leader]"
				}
				set_leader = yes
			}
		}

##Event: Trigger 2000 Elections
country_event = {
 id = TurkeyPresidency.1
 title = TurkeyPresidency.1.title
 desc = TurkeyPresidency.1.desc
 picture = GFX_ahmet_necdet_sezer_vs_nevzat
 fire_only_once = yes

    trigger = {
		tag = TUR
         AND = {
             date > 2000.2.7
	         date < 2000.8.7
         }
		 		 has_elections = yes
		 NOT = {
		 has_global_flag = ATA_NO_PR_ALLOWED
		 has_government = fascism
		 has_global_flag = TUR_MAFIATIC_TR
		 has_capitulated = yes
		 } 
    }

    mean_time_to_happen = {days = 1}

	option = {
		name = TurkeyPresidency.1.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}
    
clr_global_flag = END_THE_ELECTION_PR
	set_variable = { var = TUR_the_first_party value = 38 }
	set_variable = { var = TUR_the_second_party value = 62 }

	clr_global_flag = RTE_make_visible
	clr_global_flag = NECDET_make_visible
	clr_global_flag = EKMELEDDIN_make_visible
	clr_global_flag = SABAHATTIN_make_visible
	clr_global_flag = ABDULLAH_GUL_make_visible
	clr_global_flag = NEVZAT_make_visible

	set_global_flag = NEVZAT_make_visible
	set_global_flag = NECDET_make_visible

} }

		update_government_coalition_strength = yes
		clear_coalition_count_ideas = yes

			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { party_pop_array^23 = 1.0 }
				recalculate_party = yes
			}

add_named_threat = { threat = 3 name = TURKISH_REVOLUTION_OTT }

			clear_array = ruling_party
			clear_array = gov_coalition_array
			add_to_array = { ruling_party = 23 } #Monarchist
			add_to_variable = { party_pop_array^23 = 0.5 } #Monarchist
			recalculate_party = yes
			IF = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_government = nationalist
					}
				}
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = nationalist
					popularity = 0.5
				}
				recalculate_party = yes
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = nationalist
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_ruling_leader = yes
				set_leader = yes
			}
		

                set_politics = {
                ruling_party = nationalist
                elections_allowed = no
            }	
			
	retire_character = hursit_tolon
	retire_character = TUR_izzet_cetingoz
	retire_character = TUR_abidin_unal	
	retire_character = sener_eruygur	
	set_global_flag = CIVILWAR_OF_OTTOMANS
	hidden_effect = {
	add_timed_idea = { idea = turkish_civil_war_modifier days = 215}   
		KTU = {
add_timed_idea = { idea = turkish_civil_war_modifier days = 215}   
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = {
	KTU = {
	transfer_state = 156
	transfer_state = 157
	transfer_state = 161
	add_state_core = 156
	add_state_core = 157
	add_state_core = 161

    add_state_core = 156

    add_state_core = 163

    add_state_core = 162

	load_OOB = TUR_civilwar_2
	load_OOB = TUR_civilwar_2_air
	         declare_war_on = { target = MTU type = annex_everything }
	} }

country_event = {
 id = TurkeyC.2
 title = TurkeyC.2.title
 desc = TurkeyC.2.desc
 picture = GFX_kemalist_guards
 fire_only_once = yes

    trigger = {
         tag = TUR
		has_global_flag = TUR_CIVILWAR_BEGIN
	TUR = {
	has_war_with = MTU
	 }
	NOT = {
		 has_capitulated = yes
		 }
    }

    mean_time_to_happen = {days = 1}

	option = {
		name = TurkeyC.2.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}
    
	retire_character = hursit_tolon
	retire_character = sener_eruygur
	retire_character = TUR_abidin_unal	
	set_global_flag = war_on_kemalists

    hidden_effect = {
		MTU = {
add_timed_idea = { idea = turkish_civil_war_modifier days = 125}   
		}
	} }
	hidden_effect = {
	remove_ideas = turkish_military_infulence
	remove_ideas = turkish_military_infulence_coup
	remove_ideas = turkish_military_infulence_armed
	KTU = {
	transfer_state = 156
	transfer_state = 157
	transfer_state = 161
	add_state_core = 156
	add_state_core = 157
	add_state_core = 161

    add_state_core = 156
    add_state_core = 157
    add_state_core = 160
    add_state_core = 159
	add_state_core = 1205
    add_state_core = 163
    add_state_core = 935
    add_state_core = 162
	add_state_core = 934
	add_state_core = 155
	add_state_core = 992
	add_state_core = 1032
		
	load_OOB = TUR_civilwar_2
                load_OOB = TUR_civilwar_1_air
	         declare_war_on = { target = MTU type = annex_everything }
    	         declare_war_on = { target = KTU type = annex_everything }

	option = {

	}

##Event: MIT Rebels
country_event = {
 id = TurkeyM.1
 title = TurkeyM.1.title
 desc = TurkeyM.1.desc
 picture = GFX_republican_propaganda
 is_triggered_only = yes

    trigger = {
    }

    mean_time_to_happen = {days = 1}

	option = {
		name = TurkeyM.1.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 100}

        hidden_effect = {
	remove_ideas = pr_necdet
set_country_flag = MIT_FOCUS_LOCATION  
	add_to_array = { ruling_party = 14 }
	
		hidden_effect = {
			clear_array = gov_coalition_array
			clear_array = ruling_party
			add_to_array = { ruling_party = max_index_1 }
		}

		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				set_country_flag = [META_SET_PARTY1]
			}
			META_SET_PARTY1 = "[meta_set_ideology1]"
		}

		set_leader = yes #set in desicion which party is selected
		update_government_coalition_strength = yes
		clear_coalition_count_ideas = yes

			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { party_pop_array^14 = 1.0 }
				recalculate_party = yes
			}

add_named_threat = { threat = 3 name = MIT_REVOLUTION_OTT }
				hidden_effect = { news_event = Turkey_news.49 } 

			clear_array = ruling_party
			clear_array = gov_coalition_array
			add_to_array = { ruling_party = 15 } #Neutrality
			add_to_variable = { party_pop_array^15 = 0.5 } #Neutrality
			recalculate_party = yes
			IF = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_government = neutrality
					}
				}
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = neutrality
					popularity = 0.5
				}
				recalculate_party = yes
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = neutrality
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_ruling_leader = yes
				set_leader = yes
			}
		

                set_politics = {
                ruling_party = neutrality
                elections_allowed = no
            }	
			
	set_global_flag = MIT_RISES_UP
    clr_global_flag = NO_MIT
    set_global_flag = YES_MIT
	clr_global_flag = YES_TUR_MAFIATIC_TR
	set_global_flag = NO_TUR_MAFIATIC_TR

	create_country_leader = {
		name = "Hakan Fidan"
		picture = "hakan_fidan.dds"
		ideology = oligarchism
		traits = {
			oligarchism
			spy
		} 
	} 


    hidden_effect = {
add_timed_idea = { idea = turkish_civil_war_modifier days = 365}   		
		WTU = {
add_timed_idea = { idea = turkish_civil_war_modifier days = 365}   
		}
	} }
	hidden_effect = {
	WTU = {
    country_event = TurkeyAI.13
    transfer_state = 160
    transfer_state = 159
    transfer_state = 163
    transfer_state = 1032
    transfer_state = 935
    transfer_state = 162
    transfer_state = 992
    transfer_state = 158
	transfer_state = 161
	transfer_state = 157
	transfer_state = 155
	transfer_state = 934
	transfer_state = 1205

	add_state_core = 156
    add_state_core = 160
    add_state_core = 159
    add_state_core = 163
    add_state_core = 1032
    add_state_core = 935
    add_state_core = 162
    add_state_core = 992
    add_state_core = 158
	add_state_core = 161
	add_state_core = 1205
	add_state_core = 157
	add_state_core = 155
	add_state_core = 934

	load_OOB = TUR_civilwar_8
	load_OOB = TUR_civilwar_2_air
	} }
    	         declare_war_on = { target = WTU type = annex_everything }
	}
} }

			set_party_index_to_ruling_party = yes

	}
}

##Event: We Are Hearing Whispers (AKP)
country_event = {
 id = TurkeyA.1
 title = TurkeyA.1.title
 desc = TurkeyA.1.desc
 picture = GFX_armed_forces_feels_bad
 fire_only_once = yes

 trigger = {
		check_variable = { var = TUR_rel_akp value = 35 compare = less_than_or_equals }	
		has_stability < 0.2	
neutrality_Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_are_in_power = yes
		has_idea = turkish_military_infulence
 }

	option = {
		name = TurkeyA.1.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}
    
	add_stability = -0.02
	custom_effect_tooltip = MILITARY_TAKE_OVER_WARN_1
} }

 id = TurkeyA.16
 title = TurkeyA.16.title
 desc = TurkeyA.16.desc
 picture = GFX_whispers_are_finalyzing
 fire_only_once = yes

 trigger = {
		check_variable = { var = TUR_rel_akp value = -25 compare = less_than_or_equals }	
		has_stability < 0.2
		NOT = {
		OR = {
		has_idea = strikes_in_turkey
		has_idea = strikes_in_turkey_2
		has_idea = strikes_in_turkey_3
		}
		}	
neutrality_Neutral_Muslim_Brotherhood_are_in_power = yes
		has_idea = turkish_military_infulence
 }

	option = {
		name = TurkeyA.16.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}
    
	country_event = TurkeyF.1
        AZE = { change_tag_from = TUR }	  
} }

##Event: AI CHP
country_event = {
 id = TurkeyAI.2
 title = TurkeyAI.2.title
 desc = TurkeyAI.2.desc
 picture = GFX_the_great_ai
 fire_only_once = yes

 trigger = {
 tag = TUR
 is_ai = yes
	 has_game_rule = {
     rule = TUR_ai_behavior
     option = CHP_TAKE_OVER
      } 
 }

	option = {
		name = TurkeyAI.2.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}
    
		set_country_flag = CHP_FOCUS_LOCATION
        set_country_flag = CHP_government
			load_focus_tree = MD_TUR_CHP
 TUR_kemalist_turn_ally_effect = yes 
TUR_traditionalists_turn_enemy_effect = yes 
				add_ideas = AI_powerhold_1
	hidden_effect = { remove_ideas = pr_necdet }
    hidden_effect = {
			clear_array = ruling_party
			clear_array = gov_coalition_array
			add_to_array = { ruling_party = 3 } #socialism
			add_to_variable = { party_pop_array^3 = 0.5 } #socialism
			recalculate_party = yes
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = democratic
					popularity = 0.5
				}
				recalculate_party = yes
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = democratic
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_ruling_leader = yes
				set_leader = yes
		}	}
}

				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_government = neutrality
					}

				add_popularity = {

					popularity = 0.5
				}

				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = neutrality
				}
			}
			else = {
				meta_effect = {
					text = {
						set_country_flag = [META_SET_RULING_PARTY]
					}
					META_SET_RULING_PARTY = "[meta_set_ruling_leader]"
				}
				set_leader = yes
			}
			}
		}

country_event = {
 id = TurkeyAI.14
 title = TurkeyAI.14.title
 desc = TurkeyAI.14.desc
 picture = GFX_the_great_ai
 fire_only_once = yes

 trigger = {
 tag = TUR
 is_ai = yes
	 has_game_rule = {
     rule = TUR_ai_behavior
     option = AYM_TAKE_OVER
      } 
 }

	option = {
		name = TurkeyAI.14.a
		ai_chance = { factor = 1}

   set_global_flag = COURT_RULES_AYM
   clr_global_flag = MP_MOMENT
        set_country_flag = AYM_government
				add_ideas = AI_powerhold_4
 TUR_kemalist_turn_ally_effect = yes 
TUR_traditionalists_turn_enemy_effect = yes 
	set_variable = { var = TUR_united_aym value = 55 }
	set_variable = { var = TUR_politicized_aym value = 45 }
	set_variable = { var = TUR_soft_group value = 3 }
	set_variable = { var = TUR_precision_group value = 7 }
	set_variable = { var = TUR_transparent_group value = 5 }
		set_country_flag = AYM_FOCUS_LOCATION
        hidden_effect = { news_event = Turkey_news.35 }
	hidden_effect = { remove_ideas = pr_necdet }
				load_focus_tree = MD_TUR_AYM

	create_country_leader = {
		name = "Constitutional Court"
		picture = "aym_governance.dds"
		ideology = Neutral_conservatism
		traits = {
			neutrality_Neutral_conservatism
			staunch_constitutionalist
		}
	}
		clear_array = ruling_party
			clear_array = gov_coalition_array
			add_to_array = { ruling_party = 14 } #Neutral_conservatism
			add_to_variable = { party_pop_array^14 = 0.5 } #Neutral_conservatism
			recalculate_party = yes
			IF = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_government = neutrality
					}
				}
				add_popularity = {
					ideology = neutrality
					popularity = 0.5
				}
				recalculate_party = yes
				set_politics = {
					ruling_party = neutrality
				}
			}
			else = {
				meta_effect = {
					text = {
						set_country_flag = [META_SET_RULING_PARTY]
					}
					META_SET_RULING_PARTY = "[meta_set_ruling_leader]"
				}
				set_leader = yes
			}
		}   
	}
